description: "Adopted frozen Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.14 at readiness, closing the previously unauthored Urgent/Hidden quest gap. Non-daily System-quest capacity is now 1 by default, 2 with Multitask, and 3 with Analyst; Daily and class quests use reserved slots. Urgent quests now require an actionable nearby life-threatening crisis, explicit acceptance, a causal deadline, and fixed XP equal to four times the Bearer's System-Rank common-kill value. Hidden quests now require a pre-authored concealed discovery and a stored reveal/objective/completion contract, attach automatically when capacity permits, reveal only through in-fiction action, and award the Bearer's attachment-Rank Gate-clear milestone XP. Both types now have exact capacity, abandonment, settlement, storage, and rendering rules. Alexander's base Intelligence is 30 after `EVT-000114`, so Multitask initializes his live non-daily capacity to 2 with no active quests and no pending offers. No old campaign objective was converted, no missed opportunity was reconstructed, and no fictional time, XP, reward, Stat, pool, item, skill, daily quest, or resolved outcome changed."
```

## EVT-000116 - The Crew's First Training Session, and Telling Them

```yaml
id: EVT-000116
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: gameplay
  game_date: "2026-08-02, 16:20 to 21:50"
  real_date: "2026-07-26T19:05:32+02:00"
type: Event
kind: gameplay
importance: major
game_date: "2026-08-02 21:50 -05:00"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000136
  - ENT-000137
  - ENT-000138
  - ENT-000139
description: "The first exercise of the tactical command Priya Okafor ceded on 2026-07-29, and the evening that followed it. No monster was fought and no XP was earned; everything below is training, assessment, and relationship canon.

**Getting there.** Alexander left his studio on foot with the dossier folder under his arm. Owen offered to collect him by car at 17:15; Alexander answered with a dropped pin and *Are you seriously going by a CAR to a TRAINING*, and Owen replied that his keys were back on the hook and he was taking the Red Line. **Ruling (owner, this session): that was theatre.** Owen had in fact already driven his Civic to the Coalition, parked it, and then ridden the Red Line back in the wrong direction so that the two of them would arrive on foot together — roughly twenty-five minutes each way, a fact he conceded only at the end of the night. They met at the platform, walked in together, and Alexander asked one favour before they reached the field: that Owen hold any announcement about the relationship until after the training, so nothing was being discussed before the drill. Owen agreed, on the condition that *after* meant tonight rather than a policy.

**The briefing.** Delivered standing, from memory, folder unopened and never shown. Priya and Julian anchor a front line built around her aggression and his fire discipline; Renata is freed to read rather than fight; Owen stands **on** the line in sight of every body that can be hurt, is told the instant anyone is hurt by call rather than by observation, and the call on when he spends a repair belongs to the field commander. Priya identified it immediately as the Ashfield formation gap. Julian accepted his fire lanes in four words. Owen heard his own three operating terms delivered to the crew as doctrine, uncredited and unsoftened. Alexander did not tell them, or imply, that he was assessing them.

**The sparring — light, unarmed, no blunted weapons, in order of combat experience.**

- **Priya Okafor (D-Rank).** Her opening three combinations were read clean (d100 33, net +2, effective 73, success): honest boxing, hip behind everything, and a habit of letting her weight travel past the end of the third beat with the guard already dropping. Alexander then took that half-beat deliberately (d100 15, net +3, effective 75, success), stepping inside the hook, taking the wrist and crossing the collarbone to put her flat and controlled, her boxer's bridge-and-escape failing to move him at all. **This is the first explicitly demonstrated material application of the stat-milestone skill Overpower** (grapple/pin up to one Rank above his System Rank; Priya is D-Rank). She demanded the diagnosis in front of her own crew rather than a rematch, took it, and said the habit dates to age twenty-four.
- **Julian Boyd (E-Rank).** Waited, planted, and threw one enormous straight right a third of a second late; Alexander slipped inside to zero range where reach and a shotgun are worth nothing and put him down (d100 75, net +3, effective 95, strong success). The useful finding was not the takedown: when his single idea failed, his system stopped — no shove, no clinch, no attempt to reset distance. That is the exact question Alexander had written on his page (never tested against a plan that failed mid-execution), answered in under two seconds. Alexander's prescription: not boxing, but a **second answer** that buys two metres. He also committed the crew to a **range session next time**, and asked Julian what his ranked shotgun does that another gun does not — a question nobody has ever asked him.
- **Renata Marchetti (E-Rank).** Declined to be shown how to beat him and asked what she was meant to learn. Alexander told her he would defend only and instructed her to come at him fully; she produced six years of police control tactics — wrist and elbow entries, level changes, walking him toward the bench, forearm and knee when that failed — and touched him zero times across roughly forty seconds (d100 66, net +3, effective 100, **legendary success**). Findings: she does not attack, she **selects** — waiting for a specific configuration and committing totally, the same instinct that made her reads decisive at Ashfield — and her one hole is that she is wired to **stop** a threat rather than kill one, twice taking a control option over an ending one. Correct against a person, a wasted opening against a beast.
- **Owen Callahan (C-Rank mender).** Alexander did not spar him. He taught him for roughly eight minutes in front of the crew — fist and stacked wrist, power off the back foot, then turning Owen's own clinical knowledge of where a body comes apart into targeting, then the environment as a weapon. Then he told Owen to come at him for real, and **stood still and did not defend**. Owen's first attempt stopped four inches short by reflex — *my arm just stopped* — the mender's wiring, stated aloud. Told plainly that he was counting on him not to stop next time, Owen reset and threw it properly: **d100 48, net +1, effective 68, success.**

**The punch, and the point of it.** Owen's strike landed on Alexander's jaw and dealt Rank-honest damage under Section 6.2 — a C-Rank hunter's unarmed strike (250 / 4 = 62.5, x0.5 chassis, x1 standard result) against ~23% equipped physical reduction: **24 damage, Health 116 to 92/116.** No injury tier was reached (the hit removed neither half his remaining Health nor took him below 25% of maximum, Section 6.3). Alexander then asked to be fixed, and Owen's resolved treatment touch restored the loss outright — **Health 92 to 116/116** — an ordinary off-Gate treatment rather than a per-clear touch inside a Gate. Two things were established at once, in front of the whole crew: that Owen can strike when told to and undo it afterwards, and that **C-Rank is a real magnitude in a man everyone had filed as support.** Priya, Julian, and Renata all reacted to it out loud; Renata's line was *he's C-Rank, we keep saying it like it's a certificate.* Alexander smiled at him openly — the second time this crew has seen him smile, both times at Owen.

**The formation walk (roughly 18:45 to 21:00).** Walked at pace, not run, on Priya's request. Positions physically occupied; fire lanes marked with a bottle and a spare marker; the call form fixed as *who, what, where*, said aloud, which took three passes to break a month of the crew's habit of silently continuing while hurt. Renata ran her own proposal — deliberate sabotage, calling a threat late and then not at all — and the formation folded in two different ways, teaching more than the three clean passes before it. Priya spent the last half hour drilling the stopped third beat and swearing at her own hips. The spend-call rule went in last and Owen said *yeah* to it in front of the crew without looking at Alexander. **Next session set: same field, same time, next Sunday (2026-08-09), unless a job lands first.**

**Crew business.** Priya raised that the crew has no contract and does not exist between jobs. Alexander's standing order: she watches the board and the desk and calls him when something worth having appears, and **at least one further training happens before this crew enters another Gate.** Priya agreed and said she would have argued for two, and that she will read the assessment confidence properly this time rather than believing the posted number.

**Telling them (`REL-000066`, `REL-000065`).** With the field lights going out, Owen told Priya at ordinary volume, in front of Julian and Renata, that he and Alexander are together and have been about a week. Priya's recalculation was visible and Owen enjoyed every frame of it; Julian said *yeah, alright*; Renata said only *the stretches* — she had assembled it earlier in the evening from Alexander's offhand remark that he had already shown Owen stretching moves, and from the smile. Priya congratulated both of them and then, as contract lead, asked the professional question once, out loud, deliberately on a training field rather than in a Gate: does the spend-call rule hold when it is Owen bleeding, and when it is Alexander bleeding. Alexander answered by pointing at the punch. Priya accepted it as an answer to Owen's half — *he'll do the thing that's needed even when he hates it* — and stated plainly that Alexander's half remains untested and that she only asks that he has thought about it before it happens.

**Renata's question, deferred.** Earlier, after her spar, Renata put it directly: the filed report says Alexander was the support striker who got hurt and was treated, and she has now watched him take apart a D-Rank in two seconds and go untouched for forty. *Who are you, exactly?* Alexander answered *Now I am your trainer*, deferred personal questions to later, and moved on. She let it go visibly by choice rather than by being deflected. **This is the first direct inquiry into Alexander's capability by anyone other than Owen** (`OBJ-10`) and it is social rather than investigative — but it is on the record and she did not forget it.

**Afterwards.** Alone under the last floodlights, Alexander told Owen he was proud of him; Owen — who has never been told that about this work in five years — did not produce his usual three-seconds-later joke and got there wet-eyed instead. Alexander added that Owen is the most capable of growing of the four of them together, and Owen declined to deflect it, answering *say that to me again in a month and I'll believe it.*

**The crew question, advanced but not decided (`OBJ-12`).** Owen volunteered where his thinking actually is: he does not know how to lead, and fears a structure where his name is on the postings and Alexander's voice runs the field; and tonight killed half his own argument, because the reason to leave was that no crew ever puts the mender in the plan and Alexander just put him in the plan, inside Priya's crew, in front of Priya. **He will not decide until he has seen this crew actually run the formation on a real job.** Alexander answered with his own position for the first time: he does not see himself staying in this crew long-term — they are not bad hunters or bad people, but the command shift he has already caused will, in his judgement, eventually explode with Priya — and he explicitly refused to use that to recruit Owen, telling him to stay if he is genuinely happy there, that this is not his first crew, and that *I will still be in your bed; that's not going to change any decision.*

**Close.** They drove to Rogers Park in Owen's car — Alexander choosing Owen's apartment on the grounds that all his things are already in it — arriving around 21:50. Nothing is owed to the System, the crew, or anyone else until the daily quest issues at 06:00 on 2026-08-03.

**Settlement at this event.** Campaign-time anchor advanced `2026-08-02T16:20:00-05:00` to `2026-08-02T21:50:00-05:00`. Mana recovered from 43/60 to **60/60** across the interval (active mode while out and moving; remainder zeroed on reaching full) and both recovery modes are `resting` with zero carry at the new anchor. Health returned to **116/116** after the punch and the treatment. No XP, gold, cash, item, or equipment change; a coffee bought with cash is immaterial to the tracked balance. **Overpower successful material applications 0 to 1.** No identifier other than this event was minted."
```

## EVT-000117 - Profile 1.15: The High-Rank Sponsor Exception

```yaml
id: EVT-000117
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: owner-ruling
  game_date: "2026-08-03 06:05 -05:00; no fictional time consumed"
  real_date: "2026-07-26T19:05:32+02:00"
type: Event
kind: system-profile-adoption
importance: structural
game_date: "2026-08-03 06:05 -05:00; no fictional time consumed"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
description: "Adopted frozen Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.15, an additive party-composition advance over 1.14, on an owner ruling made when the question 'is there an official way for two hunters to take an E-Rank Gate' met a profile that answered only 'lawful, but no board will post it.'

**The new clause (Section 9.4, the high-Rank sponsor exception).** A **confirmed E-Rank** contract may be posted to and bid by a party of **any size** when at least one member holds a **C-Rank or higher** licence; a **confirmed D-Rank** contract likewise behind a **B-Rank or higher** licence. Two owner rulings fix its edges: it applies to **confirmed assessments only** — an unconfirmed Gate keeps the ordinary posting floors of 8, or 5 under the under-strength waiver, so a sponsored pair can never be surprised by a true Rank two bands above them the way the Ashfield crew was — and a sponsored posting is **sanctioned, therefore insured**, with the solo-entry insurance void unchanged for anyone crossing outside such a posting.

Legal minimums are untouched (Section 13.3 already required no combat-rated minimum at E-Rank–D-Rank); the clause moves the board's floor rather than the law's. Standard contract terms, exit declaration, liability waivers, and beneficiary rules apply unchanged, and the sponsor takes no automatic leader's share. The trade's opinion of thin crews is explicitly left intact.

**Consequence for this campaign.** Owen Callahan (`ENT-000139`) is **C-Rank**, which makes a two-person confirmed-E-Rank clear — Owen sponsoring, Alexander fighting — a legal, postable, insured job for the first time. No such contract has been sought or signed as of this event.

**Nothing recomputes.** No fictional time, Stat, pool, XP, gold, item, skill, quest, streak, contract, filed report, or resolved outcome changed. Immutable checkpoints through `900_CHECKPOINT_0026` remain byte-unchanged at Profile 1.14 and run the additive 1.14 to 1.15 adoption at readiness on restore."
```

## EVT-000118 - The Sponsor Contract: A Gate for Two, and Owen's First Gear

```yaml
id: EVT-000118
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: gameplay
  game_date: "2026-08-03, 06:00 to 09:40"
  real_date: "2026-07-26T20:05:00+02:00"
type: Event
kind: gameplay
importance: major
game_date: "2026-08-03 09:40 -05:00"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000139
  - ENT-000130
  - ENT-000133
description: "The morning after the crew's first training session, and the first job Alexander has ever taken that exists because of somebody else's licence.

**06:00 — rotation and the daily.** Woke at Owen Callahan's apartment to the tenth daily quest and the Daily Premium rotation. The **2026-08-02 Umbral cycle expired 0/6**, taking the Adaptive Titan Torso [C-Rank] he had been 1,480 g short of. The new **2026-08-03 cycle rolled Verdant** (d8 5) with real draws: weapon **Farline Longshot [D-Rank]** (d100 79 → +1 Rank; 900 g), armor **Adaptive Bastion Torso [E-Rank]** (d100 25 → own Rank; 120 g, a duplicate of the piece he wears), accessory **Ascendant Hunter's Band [D-Rank]** (d100 56 → +1 Rank; 720 g, a duplicate of an equipped Band), consumable **Sovereign Panacea** (600 g), rune **Premium Rune teaching Sprint [E-Rank]** (d100 12 → own Rank, d10 1 → Sprint, unknown so no reroll; 2,500 g), key **Runic Key [E-Rank]** (d100 5 → own Rank; 1,000 g).

**The Runic Key, bought at a cost.** Gold stood at 920. He **sold 6 Lesser Healing Potions** at the 50% resale rate (12 g each, +72 g → 992) and the **Quickstep Charm [E-Rank]** at the 25% equipment rate (+20 g → 1,012), then **bought the Runic Key for 1,000 g** — its boss drops one guaranteed rune in addition to the normal boss-drop roll. **Gold 1,012 → 12.** Potions 10 → 4; the Charm is withdrawn permanently. The Daily Premium cycle stands at **5/6 remaining** until the 2026-08-04 06:00 rotation.

**The tenth daily.** Run and calisthenics on the Rogers Park lakefront with Owen for the fifth consecutive morning — Owen holding Saturday's loop and swearing through the last two kilometres rather than stopping, plus a scaled 60 of each. Completed by roughly 07:50. **Streak 9 → 10**; not a multiple of seven, so the box is not streak-upgraded (next eligible at 14). Rewards pending as three separate entries: **Ability Points +3 ×1, Status Recovery ×5 (four carried plus this one), Daily Random Box ×1.**

**The proposal, and the profile that had to move for it.** Over breakfast Alexander proposed a two-person official E-Rank clear. The governing rule at the time (Section 9.4) made it lawful but unbookable — legal minimum none at E-Rank–D-Rank, board posting floor 8 or 5 under waiver. The owner ruled the **high-Rank sponsor exception** into the profile (see `EVT-000117`, Profile 1.15): a confirmed E-Rank contract may be staffed at any party size behind a C-Rank licence. **Owen is C-Rank.** That single fact converts a thing Alexander could only do illegally-in-spirit into a posted, insured job.

**What Alexander actually said it was for.** Asked directly whether this was training, money, or something else, he answered: *I want to show you… myself, I suppose. To answer your questions with actually no need to answer them.* Owen named back exactly what that means — that a live Gate interior admits no cameras and no instruments (Section 13.4), so it is the one room on Earth where Alexander can stop being careful — and made him say the consequence out loud before accepting: that Owen would stop being the man politely not asking and become the man who **knows**, and would carry that around Priya, Renata, and every desk officer in Chicago. Alexander's answer was two words: ***I trust you.*** Owen said yes.

**The rule of engagement, written by the mender.** Owen refused a safe corner and refused a vague promise. He proposed and Alexander accepted an exact line: **Owen takes one beast alone; if a second engages him at the same time, Alexander ends it; and if Owen is hurt badly enough to want a touch on himself, he says the word aloud and Alexander ends it, with no vote for Owen at that moment** — his own reasoning being that the man asking to be patched always gets that call wrong, the same principle he gave the crew for the spend-call.

**The contract (`ENT-000130`, Kesha Morrison).** Owen rang the Coalition desk as **sponsor of record** and confirmed the **Cicero service-tunnel Gate under a decommissioned substation — confirmed E-Rank, clean assessment**, sat unbid over a week because the posted rate is insulting split eight ways. Declined: a flooded basement level under a West Town parking structure, same money, with a residential mouth and daily calls from the neighbourhood association — visibility Alexander did not want. Terms: **sponsor of record Owen Callahan, no leader's share, 50/50 split, flat posted rate, loot declared at exit, licences scanned to Kesha before 11:00, at the mouth by 13:00.** On instruction, and in front of the desk, Owen also told Kesha that Alexander is his boyfriend; her reply was that it was about time and that the pair of them had been insufferable on the phone for a week. **Kesha Morrison now knows about the relationship.**

**Money.** The **≈$9,450 Ashfield share landed** (BGM Region V clear settlement, filed true by Priya Okafor on 2026-07-30): **cash $18,881 → $28,331.** The ≈$9,000 Sable & Kern harvest share is still processing on its first business day.

**Owen's first protective gear, bought at Horizon Outfitters (`ENT-000133`).** The agreed split of cost — gloves from Alexander, the rest from Owen — did not survive Owen choosing E-Rank kit for himself. Alexander overrode it and bought all three pieces:

- **Horizon Bastion Gauntlets [C-Rank]** — hands, physical reduction 8%, $12,500. Owen's first thought on wearing them was that they do not deaden the hands, and that he had spent five years not buying gloves on the assumption that anything real would put a wall between his hands and the person he was treating.
- **Horizon Bastion Torso [D-Rank]** — torso, physical reduction 5%, $3,000.
- **Horizon Bastion Boots [D-Rank]** — feet, physical reduction 5%, $3,000.

**Cash $28,331 → $15,831 → $9,831.** The shop's clerk stated the governing fact plainly: an ordinary licensed hunter has no five-Stat sheet (Section 11.5), so ranked gear buys him **coverage, not Stats** — the percentages are the whole benefit. Owen bought his own consumables and field kit out of stubbornness, objected in earnest that the override took away the one part of the day that was his, and extracted a counter-term: he buys lunch. Alexander's reply — ***you're buying lunch for the rest of your life*** — is the mirror of the standing breakfast arrangement from `EVT-000111`, and Owen identified it as such immediately.

**Standing at the end of this event.** 2026-08-03, roughly 09:40, on Michigan Avenue. Level 8, XP 400/800, Health 116/116, Mana 60/60, no injury, gold **12**, cash **$9,831**, daily streak **10**, non-daily System quest slots 0/2. One unused **Runic Key [E-Rank]**. A confirmed E-Rank Gate at 13:00 in Cicero, with a C-Rank mender wearing real armour for the first time in his life."
```

## EVT-000119 - The Cicero Gate: Entry and the Corridor Clear

```yaml
id: EVT-000119
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-3-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-08-03, ~13:00-14:00"
  real_date: "2026-07-26T23:58:12+02:00"
type: Event
kind: session-gameplay
importance: moderate
game_date: "2026-08-03, ~13:00-14:00"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000139
description: "After a quiet midday at Owen's apartment — real food, licences filed well ahead of Kesha's eleven o'clock deadline, both fully rested — the two of them rode the Blue Line to Cicero and entered the confirmed E-Rank service-tunnel Gate at 13:00. The interior resolved alien and den-scented; archetype rolled **Beast Den** (d8 5) — two boss-Rank alphas instead of one — and population rolled **8 common E-Rank / 1 elite / 2 boss-Rank** (3d6=8, 1d2=1).

**The corridor.** Alexander read the ground for an isolated first target (d100 37, net +1, effective 57, partial success): a lone beast down the right passage, the read genuine but not clean — the path curved back toward the den within earshot. Activating **Keen Sense** (Mana 60→58) sharpened it on a second look (d100 33, net +2, effective 73, success): the passage's own geometry muffled sound both directions, and the lone target read as a common beast on patrol rather than anything denned. Owen took it alone on instruction — his **first solo kill**, a single decisive strike (d100 93, net +2, effective legendary) — and, corrected on the spot (a common beast drops a crystal, not a core; only elites and bosses carry cores, Section 11.1), pocketed his first mana crystal.

**The delegated sweep.** Alexander read and re-read the corridor's remaining seven commons, isolating lone targets for Owen and engaging clustered groups himself, leaving one from each cluster for Owen to finish: Alexander's two pockets (d100 97 legendary natural; d100 73→legendary), Owen's two solo kills (d100 40→success with a minor graze his gauntlet absorbed; d100 66→legendary). All seven cleared; no injury tier on either hunter. Seven more crystals banked between them.

**The elite.** A tactical read from the passage mouth (d100 72, net +1 with Keen Sense still running, effective 92, strong success) resolved the den chamber ahead into two distinct alpha shapes — one broad and plated, one leaner and restless — plus a third, smaller presence denned separately near a wall gap: an elite, not yet stirred. Alexander crossed to it alone and unseen, opening with the Ghost Quickknife's first-unseen-attack bonus into an immediate Rupture (d100 68, d100 67, both legendary; Mana 58→48, +20 XP, **400→420/800**). One crystal, one core banked. No sound carried back to the pair."
```

## EVT-000120 - The Beast Den's Alpha Pair, and the Gate Cleared

```yaml
id: EVT-000120
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-3-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-08-03, ~14:00-14:30"
  real_date: "2026-07-26T23:58:12+02:00"
type: Event
kind: session-gameplay
importance: significant
game_date: "2026-08-03, ~14:00-14:30"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000139
description: "With the elite down, Alexander and Owen moved together on the den's alpha pair — a plated, Carapaced/Impact-signature anchor and a leaner, Predator/Edge-signature striker, per Rules Section 9.7's authored boss facts. A shared tactical read (d100 38, net +2 with Keen Sense, effective 78, success — hurried by the alphas already closing) identified real weak points and passed them to Owen in a handful of words: a neck seam on the plated one when it rears, a haunch-drop tell on the leaner one before it lunges.

**The opening exchange.** Both alphas closed at once, the leaner faster than its partner. Alexander opened on the read (d100 24, net +2, effective 64, success), wounding the leaner alpha with a dagger-into-Rupture combo (Mana 48→38) and taking a shallow graze in return — first real mark of the fight. Beside him, Owen met the plated alpha's opening slam by stepping inside its arc rather than bracing against it (d100 29, net +2, effective 69, success) — jarred but unhurt, the new gear absorbing the impact.

**The leaner alpha falls.** Denying it any chance to reset, Alexander pressed immediately with both daggers and a second Rupture (d100 59, net +3, effective legendary; Mana 38→28, +40 XP, **420→460/800**) — dead in two exchanges. The plated alpha, still resetting from its own slam, watched its mate die and stopped posturing.

**The plated alpha falls, and the Gate clears.** Alexander closed the distance with Flash Step before it could reset again (Mana 21 after cost 7), landing the neck-seam strike (d100 80, net +3, effective legendary) in the same beat Owen struck from his own angle (d100 94, net +2, effective legendary). It didn't get up. **Gate cleared** — no threshold crossed on the +40 boss-kill XP plus the 70 XP clear milestone (**460→570/800**).

**Boss drops.** The leaner alpha rolled a skill book (d100 96, 91–97; d10 10) — **Bulwark [E-Rank]**, learned, a skill Alexander did not already know. The plated alpha rolled equipment (d100 28, 01–40; d20 20 — the drop's own open-choice result) — a **Boss-Imprinted Quickknife [E-Rank]**, chassis chosen as a weapon at the player's discretion, composed from a d6 finish and the plated alpha's own Carapaced/Impact imprint (the body-family Vitality point inapplicable to an ordinary hunter's frame per Section 11.5; the Impact Echo — +1 modifier step, once per scene, on a Strength action to break, shove, or resist forced movement — live regardless). Drawn from the dimensional inventory and put directly into Owen's hand: his first weapon. Two crystals, two cores banked from the pair."
```

## EVT-000121 - Exit, the Core Declaration, and the Truth About Pocket Gates

```yaml
id: EVT-000121
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"

  source: session-3-gameplay

kind: session-gameplay
importance: significant
game_date: "2026-08-03, ~14:30-16:00"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000139
description: "With the two-hour clear window open, neither of them worked the wall deposits — this wasn't a mining trip. Genuine haul from the whole clear: **11 E-Rank crystals** (one per beast, 8 commons + 1 elite + 2 alphas) and **3 E-Rank cores** (elite and both alphas, the only core-capable kills the Gate actually held). Declared under the sponsor contract's standard 50/50 terms.

**The core declaration.** Before the desk, Alexander pulled Owen aside and asked him to fold all eleven of his private, unregulated E-Rank cores into the report — cores with no Gate attached to them at all. Owen refused blind: *where are eleven cores with no Gate actually from.* Told the truth — a private, solo-only form of Gate, informally 'a pocket gate,' that only Alexander can open, entirely off the books — he took it steadily, connected it to everything he'd already filed without asking, and agreed to help on one condition: the number had to be plausible, not the whole eleven. They settled on **2 extra cores** (5 declared total). Owen called Kesha Morrison and corrected the report live; she accepted it without further question (d100 83, net +2, effective legendary — nothing external exists to check a number against, per Section 13.4's Witness Rule, so a statistically ordinary figure passes cleanly).

**Declared and sold:** 11 crystals ($1,650) + 5 cores ($1,875) = **$3,525**, split 50/50 — **≈$1,762.50 each**, processing 1–2 business days. Alexander's private stash: **11 cores → 9**, the rest still with no legal sale path in either economy.

Owen, closing the call: *That's the last one I do on instinct. Next time, the math comes first.* A kiss on Michigan Avenue, and the walk back to Owen's apartment for the evening — nothing owed to the System, the crew, or anyone else until the next daily issues at 06:00 on 2026-08-04.

**Standing at close.** Level 8, XP **570/800**, Health 116/116, Mana **21/60**, no injury. Gold 12, cash $9,831, pending ≈$1,762.50 from this Gate. Nine skills plus the newly learned Bulwark. Owen owns his first weapon."
```

## EVT-000122 - Damen Avenue, and the First Kiss at the Door

```yaml
id: EVT-000122
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-3-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-08-03, ~16:15"
  real_date: "2026-07-27T11:30:00+02:00"
type: Event
kind: session-gameplay
importance: significant
game_date: "2026-08-03, ~16:15"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000139
description: "At Owen's door, asked plainly whether he was good, Alexander answered honestly and for the first time volunteered why the answer mattered: since his parents died at the **Damen Avenue Break** — the first fully documented American dungeon break, July 2016, ~400 dead, established world canon (`worlds/gatefall/200_WORLD_BIBLE.md` Timeline) — this is the first time he has been genuinely, simply good.

Alexander was fifteen at the time, turning sixteen later that year — consistent with the personal-history note already on record (`100_CHARACTER_SHEET.md`: 'about fifteen when the Gatefall happened... fifteen still when the Damen Avenue Break killed some four hundred people in his own city'). Both his parents had awakened in the Gatefall's opening week (March 2016) — his mother a university professor, his father an FBI Task Force detective. When the Break hit, his mother was caught in it; his father, newly awakened and with no BGM protocol yet chartered to guide or support him (the Bureau of Gate Management would not exist for another year, per the World Bible Timeline), fought to reach her, killed several of the things pouring out, and was killed alongside her by something above what an untrained week-old awakening could survive.

Owen took the name of the street and said nothing else for a moment, then pulled Alexander inside by the front of his jacket rather than push for more, and told him to stay as long as he wanted — 'tonight' amended a moment later, under a small tease, to no fixed limit at all. The two of them kissed at the threshold, unhurried, before going in. **Alexander's exact birthdate is fixed the same evening: September 17, 2000** — consistent with the established 'fifteen, turning sixteen' framing and his current stated age of 25 as of this session's anchor; he turns 26 later this year. Coincidentally three days after Owen's own birthday (14 September, `EVT-000108`), unnoticed by either of them in the moment.

This is the first time Alexander has told anyone in this campaign why he is the way he is."
```

Owen pointed out the real logistics (another confirmed E/D-Rank posting, his own card as sponsor of record each time, Kesha Morrison's patience) but didn't say no.

Alexander then offered to actually show Owen how the private cores come from — a solo instant-dungeon clear, done together — and was careful to correct the frame Owen might reasonably assume: a pocket-gate clear pays nothing in USD and produces only cores with no legal provenance, exactly the kind that forced the Cicero Gate's declaration problem in the first place (`EVT-000121`). Starting to say he'd need to find a key first, he caught himself mid-sentence: he already has the unused **Runic Key [E-Rank]** banked from that morning's Daily Premium purchase (`EVT-000118`), forgotten in the bag since. The demonstration Owen was promised 'soon' is therefore available whenever either of them chooses to use it, not pending a future drop or purchase — recorded as a live thread continuing OBJ-13.

No Stat, XP, gold, or item change. The Runic Key remains unused, banked."

## EVT-000128 - Profile 1.17: Instructional Binding and Recipient Settlement

```yaml
id: EVT-000128
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: ruling
  game_date: "2026-08-04 06:00 -05:00; profile migration, no fictional time consumed"
  real_date: "2026-07-27"
type: Event
kind: profile-migration
importance: major
game_date: "2026-08-04 06:00 -05:00; profile migration, no fictional time consumed"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
description: "Adopted frozen Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.17, a migrating instructional-item binding and recipient-consumption advance over 1.16.

Generation now fixes a rune or skill book's physical identity regardless of the Bearer's known skills. Ordinary catalogue and Daily Random Box instruction is Bearer-bound; Daily Premium, public-Gate boss, instant-dungeon, Runic-Key, and future found/world instruction is unbound for awakened recipients unless explicitly authored otherwise; Mastery instruction remains Bearer-bound; class instruction records its class. Eligible NPCs may learn or upgrade the original fixed technique, capped at their own fixed Rank, but receive no Stats, XP, Mana ledger, mastery track, or innate role package. Only Bearer consumption applies duplicate rerolls and the Mastery fallback.

Live migration found no held, unconsumed rune or skill book. The current Cobalt Daily Premium offer remains exactly the previously rolled **Premium Rune teaching Silent Step [C-Rank]** at 50,000 g; it receives `instruction_binding: unbound-awakened`, provenance `daily-premium` / `EVT-000126`, unused status, and the rendered label **UNBOUND · NPC-ELIGIBLE**. No roll or item identity was changed, and the offer was not rerolled. All historical consumed instruction and previously resolved rerolls — including the Stone Skin → Flash Step result — remain canon.

No fictional time, gold, item ownership, Stat, pool, skill, mastery counter, quest, purchase flag, or resolved outcome changed. Engine 0.2.0 and Data Model 0.1.4 remain unchanged. Immutable checkpoints remain byte-unchanged and run the applicable profile chain through 1.17 at readiness."

## EVT-000129 - Profile 1.18: Dual-Wield Action Packages and Twin Fang

```yaml
id: EVT-000129
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: owner-ruling
  game_date: "2026-08-04 06:00 -05:00; no fictional time consumed"
  real_date: "2026-07-27"
type: Event
kind: system-profile-adoption
importance: structural
game_date: "2026-08-04 06:00 -05:00; no fictional time consumed"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
description: "Adopted frozen Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.18, an additive dual-wield action-package and earned-technique advance over Profile 1.17.

**Owner ruling — offensive packages.** One declared offensive action selects an ordinary strike, an ordinary weapon-plus-active-skill combo, a split dual strike against two different targets, or a named multi-strike. Only one offensive active skill may occupy the action unless a named capability says otherwise. Setup, mobility, defensive, reaction, and sustained skills remain compatible when their own text permits. This preserves Alexander's established Flash Step follow-ups, dagger-plus-Rupture combinations, and the Frozen Gallery's free two-target dual strike while closing unlimited same-target attack declarations.

**Twin Fang ratified.** Alexander's successful dual-wield practice across three distinct dangerous scenes — Ashfield (`EVT-000071`), the Frozen Gallery (`EVT-000101`), and Cicero (`EVT-000120`) — satisfies Section 7.1's earned-by-doing threshold. The System grants **Twin Fang [E-Rank] ★☆☆☆☆ Novice · Mana 6**: with two equipped Quickknives, one ordinary strike against a target may be followed immediately by a separately rolled strike from the other Quickknife against that same target. Each strike uses only its own weapon power. Twin Fang occupies the action's offensive active-skill position and cannot combine with Rupture.

**Mastery.** The skill-enabled second strike begins at ×1.00 and follows the damage-skill mastery schedule ×1.00/×1.15/×1.30/×1.45/×1.60; Mana follows 6/5/5/4/4. A successful use requires the second strike to land and materially contribute. Pre-ratification evidence grants the skill but does not backfill successful uses or mastery: Twin Fang begins at uses 0, qualifying scenes 0, progress 0/3.

No fictional time, Mana, Health, XP, gold, item, quest, or resolved outcome changes. Engine 0.2.0 and Data Model 0.1.4 remain unchanged. Checkpoint 0029 stays immutable at Profile 1.16 and runs the applicable compatibility chain through 1.18 on restore."
```

## EVT-000131 - Profile 1.20: Meaningful Practice and Historical Candidate Settlement

```yaml
id: EVT-000131
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"

participants:

description: "Adopted frozen Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.20, retaining Data Model 0.1.5 and the bounded `gatefall.skill_formation` audit while extending its qualifying scenes beyond danger.

The audit never runs per attack, work roll, drill repetition, or narration response. Dangerous scenes receive one audit at `dangerous-scene-settlement`. Bounded consequential work and structured practice instead seal compact evidence notes at scene close and classify them together in one `progression-batch-settlement` Event at the next checkpoint, automatic context-preservation checkpoint, or session-close promotion barrier. That same barrier re-counts every known combat-skill activation and material passive application from the unpromoted action/resource trace, then reconciles every dangerous-scene formation audit against its retained notes and candidate mutation before writing. Non-dangerous evidence requires a declared objective, uncertain execution or real feedback, and a material result. Routine daily repetitions, uncontested rehearsal, ordinary attendance, and writing without tested application remain outside the coverage set.

**Historical candidate settlement.** Combat Pattern Exploitation becomes `pending-ratification` from the Red Line, Ashfield, and Crypt. Field Command becomes `pending-ratification` from the Red Line boss call, the Ashfield boss plan, and the Cicero alpha-pair coordination. Nonvisual Combat Mapping becomes `tracking` at 1/3 from the Crypt. Resonance Extraction becomes `pending-ratification` from the Cicero Scar, Frozen Gallery, and Halsted Depot work scenes. Formation Instruction becomes `tracking` at 1/3 from the dossier-informed crew training project. Dimensional Weapon Control becomes `tracking` at 2/3 from the private summon/grip drill and the later Ashfield pocket-swap feint. Historical Events and immutable checkpoints remain unchanged.

**Dimensional boundary and authored result.** Ordinary inventory withdrawal remains an instant, free delivery to Alexander's own hand only. It cannot place or launch an item remotely. Profile 1.20 pre-authors the result if Dimensional Weapon Control reaches its third qualifying scene: **Dimensional Projection [E-Rank]**, Mana 6, remotely deploying and launching one owned portable weapon from a visible unoccupied point within 5 metres as one ranged physical attack, with closed line-of-sight, occupancy, action-package, equipment-bonus, and mastery limits in Section 7.2. The skill is not yet granted.

No fictional time, roll, Mana, Health, XP, gold, item, quest, known skill, mastery counter, damage result, or prior outcome changes. Checkpoint 0029 remains immutable at Profile 1.16 / Data Model 0.1.4 and restores through the applicable chain to Profile 1.20 / Data Model 0.1.5."

## EVT-000132 - Profile 1.21: Three Earned Techniques Ratified

```yaml
id: EVT-000132
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: owner-ruling

participants:
  - ENT-000125
description: "Adopted frozen Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.21 and ratified three earned-by-doing candidates through explicit owner rulings.

**Exploit Pattern [E-Rank], Passive.** A successful deliberate tactical read establishes an active Pattern tied to an opponent, behavior, weakness, or structure. Every personal action genuinely exploiting it gains +1 modifier step until the scene ends or the Pattern becomes invalid. Ordinary analysis remains unrestricted; the Pattern does not stack with another modifier from the same finding. Novice supports one active Pattern, widening to 2/3/4/5 through mastery.

**Field Command [E-Rank], Passive.** Alexander may relay one active Pattern by a brief actionable call to an ally who can perceive and understand him. The ally treats it as personally understood, needs no separate tactical-read action, and gains its +1 modifier step on actions genuinely exploiting it. Alexander may update the call before the ally's roll, never after. Novice supports one recipient, widening to 2/3/4/5.

**Resonance Extraction [E-Rank], Passive.** A successful deliberate read establishes a Resonance Pattern for a mana-crystal vein. Every extraction action following it gains +1 modifier step until the vein is exhausted or materially shifts, when recalibration is required. Ordinary mining expertise remains intact. Novice benefits Alexander; mastery lets 1/2/3/4 additional miners follow his live calls.

All three begin at Novice with zero post-ratification successful uses, zero qualifying scenes, and 0/3 mastery progress. Their historical evidence established acquisition and does not backfill these counters. Candidate states become `ratified` with this Event as their resolution.

Section 7.4 now advances mastery through three distinct skill-appropriate qualifying scenes at the current level: combat skills require danger, while non-combat skills may progress through consequential work or structured practice that materially exercises their effect. This changes no historical mastery.

No fictional time, roll, Mana, Health, XP, gold, item, quest, prior skill counter, damage result, or prior outcome changes. Checkpoint 0029 remains immutable at Profile 1.16 / Data Model 0.1.4 and restores through the applicable chain to Profile 1.21 / Data Model 0.1.5."

## EVT-000136 - The Morning: A Nickname, the Complaint-Tax Run, and a Debt Named Aloud

    Woke beside Owen; sent a follow-up text to Dale Pruitt (`ENT-000135`) asking after mining work and 'the bigger thing' he'd mentioned — Dale replied that Alexander's name is genuinely under discussion, a real answer promised by Wednesday. Woke Owen with two kisses and a new pet name, 'Owwiiiie', delivered dead straight; Owen fought and lost the battle not to grin, conceded a quick coffee before the run.

    Ran the eleventh daily quest at the Rogers Park lakefront under a spontaneous house rule — one extra kilometre added to Owen's own run for every complaint he made during it — landing Owen at 13 km against his usual scaled effort (three self-inflicted complaints, three extra kilometres) while also completing his usual scaled calisthenics alongside the run. Alexander completed his own regimen (100 push-ups, 100 sit-ups, 100 squats, 10 km run) clean.

    ```
    [SYSTEM] DAILY TRAINING COMPLETE.
    Objectives complete. Rewards: Ability Points +3, Status Recovery, Daily Random Box — pending.
    Streak: 10 → 11.
    ```

    Before parting for their separate errands, Alexander told Owen plainly that he didn't want to ask him to lie again, naming the Cicero core declaration directly — the first time he has proactively volunteered discomfort about something he asked of Owen, rather than Owen naming a boundary first. Owen reframed it: he hadn't felt used, he'd felt trusted with a real choice, and confirmed that asking Kesha about an artificer contact isn't the same kind of ask, since commissioning gear requires no lie from anyone. Owen left for his own day (a rig vendor callback, Renata's rig, Coalition paperwork); Alexander noted, teasing, that Kesha likely has a soft spot for him.

    No skill-formation evidence recorded: the run and calisthenics are the routine daily checklist, explicitly outside Section 7.1's coverage set (no genuine danger, consequential work, or structured practice with a declared learning objective). No XP, gold, Mana, Health, or item change; streak advances from the completed daily quest alone.

## EVT-000138 - Wandering the City: Wade Bishop's Ironline Pitch

```yaml
id: EVT-000138
canonical_record: REC-000079

description: "Wandering the city with no plan before the 14:00 harvest staging, Alexander drifted to the Red Line Corridor (`ENT-000090`) and was approached by Ironline Guild recruiter **Wade Bishop** (`ENT-000146`, newly registered), who had heard favorable word from Ada Reyes and Dale Pruitt.

Wade pitched Ironline's structure honestly rather than polished: steady D-Rank/E-Rank strike contract flow instead of scrambling the Coalition board weekly, a real assigned crew, a gear stipend, and was candid that a guild patch does not touch the E-Rank card's contract-Rank ceiling (Profile Section 19.4) — harvest/support work remains open at any Rank regardless of guild affiliation, unchanged from freelance. Alexander declined to commit either way and instead asked for proof: a D-Rank strike trial with a **properly-Ranked crew** (not just warm bodies), and a B-Rank harvest trial to test the pitch against something real. Wade agreed to both — the D-Rank slot arrangable within the week with a verified crew, the B-Rank harvest lead needing a few days to actually surface rather than being promised on the spot — and read the added crew-Rank condition as shrewd rather than difficult. Contact card exchanged; no deadline set.

New relationship formed: `REL-000068` (pending recruitment overture, no commitment). No item, gold, Mana, Health, or XP change."
```

## EVT-000139 - The Pawn Shop: Cicero's Quiet, and the First Core Sale

```yaml
id: EVT-000139
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-3-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-08-04, ~09:30-10:15 -05:00, warehouse district"
  real_date: "2026-07-27"

participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000147
progression_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
description: "Window-shopping turned up a locked case at a warehouse-district pawn shop holding **Cicero's Quiet [C-Rank]** (Section 11.6, a named artifact: the first strike of a combat, made unseen, adds +0.5 to its skill multiplier), quoted at $35,000 with murky Cicero Scar scavenger provenance the shop's owner (`ENT-000147`) could not personally vouch for.

A through-the-glass read (🎲 d100 46, net 0, effective 46 — partial success) found the wear pattern consistent with genuine age but settled nothing about authenticity. A direct handling attempt was declared, retracted by player correction, and re-narrated as the shopkeeper granting permission only — no examination occurred on that beat. A deliberate misdirection routine followed: a showy juggling display covering a genuine dimensional-inventory store-and-recall of the dagger (🎲 d100 74, net −1 step [no showmanship training backing the cover], effective 54 — partial success). The System confirmed the item genuine on the half-second it sat in the pocket dimension — a real Cicero's Quiet, not a replica — but the pawnbroker caught an unexplained hitch in the pass and filed it away without pressing, a real complication rather than a clean deception. Not purchased.

A second, cleaner demonstration — retrieving the equipped Ghost Quickknife [B-Rank] under cover of reaching into a backpack (🎲 d100 49, net +1 step [an ordinary bag-reach needs no real trick], effective 69 — success) — read as completely mundane to the pawnbroker, who recognized the blade's quality as well above shop stock.

Before leaving, Alexander sold the pawnbroker **one private E-Rank core for $220 cash** — a genuine black-market transaction (Profile Section 12.2, felony risk both directions), reasoned from the $375 licensed anchor minus the dealer's own risk/movement discount for a first-time, no-track-record sale. The pawnbroker indicated the rate improves with demonstrated trust and repeat business.

**Cash: $9,831 → $10,051. Private E-Rank core stash: 9 → 8.** New relationship formed: `REL-000069` (informal fence contact, untested but willing). No skill-formation evidence recorded: the sleight-of-hand demonstrations were neither genuine danger, consequential work, nor structured practice with a declared learning objective — idle demonstration during downtime does not meet Section 7.1's coverage test, so `dimensional_weapon_control` remains `tracking` at 2/3, unchanged."
```

## EVT-000140 - The Rail-Corridor Harvest Job: Denise Ferro's Crew

```yaml
id: EVT-000140
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-3-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-08-04, 13:45-18:00 -05:00, warehouse-district rail corridor"
  real_date: "2026-07-27"
type: Event
kind: work
importance: immediate
game_date: "2026-08-04, 13:45-18:00 -05:00"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000148
progression_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
counter_deltas:
  - { subject: ENT-000125, counter: skills.resonance_extraction.successful_uses, delta: 2 }
  - { subject: ENT-000125, counter: skills.resonance_extraction.qualifying_scenes_total, delta: 1 }
  - { subject: ENT-000125, counter: skills.resonance_extraction.mastery_progress, delta: 1 }
description: "**Resonance Extraction mastery counters:** one continuous work shift, two successful deliberate vein reads (d100 62 net +1 effective 82 strong success; d100 45 net +1 effective 65 success). One qualifying scene, two successful applications, mastery progress 0/3 → 1/3 toward Practiced.

A confirmed C-Rank harvest slot booked by Kesha Morrison the same morning (`EVT-000137`) came due at 13:45: a fenced gravel yard off the old rail corridor, run by Denise Ferro (`ENT-000148`) for a Coalition-posted crew, two other freelancers already working their own marked sections. Standard terms: mine, declare, 80/20 split, four-hour window closing at 18:00.

Alexander's section opened with easy, routine work — two shallow seams, four crystals, no uncertainty. A thicker, deeper vein followed, and he read it deliberately before committing steel: **Resonance Extraction's first actual material use**, d100 62, net +1 step (trained technique plus the newly-ratified passive), effective 82 — strong success. The read found a clean angle through the pocket; six C-Rank crystals came free with no wasted motion or tool wear.

The rest of the section ran ordinary — fifteen more crystals mined steadily over the following hours, five sold privately to the System shop as extracted (1 in 3, per Alexander's own declared rule, 850 g total; gold 12 → 862). A second deliberate read in the last half-hour, rushed against the closing window, found one more pocket: d100 45, net +1, effective 65 — success, five more crystals, one sold (170 g; gold 862 → 1,032).

**Declared at the surface: twenty C-Rank crystals** (six kept from the good pocket, ten kept from the ordinary run, four kept from the final push). Denise counted alongside him and confirmed the tally without dispute. Standard terms: her cut 20% off the licensed rate, Alexander's net **≈$40,000, Coalition processing 1-2 business days**. New relationship formed: `REL-000068`... — new relationship `REL-000070` (harvest-hire standing with Denise Ferro's crew). One qualifying scene for Resonance Extraction (the whole shift counts once regardless of the two successful reads), mastery progress 0/3 → 1/3, Novice."

Flash Step: one qualifying scene, 2/3 → 3/3 (advances at `EVT-000144`, see below).

Away from the rail-corridor site and out of sight, Alexander used the banked **Runic Key [E-Rank]** (`EVT-000118`) to open a private instant dungeon alone. Archetype **Shattered City** (d8 6) — broken verticality, falls dealing Rank damage. Population (Section 9.7, Rank E): 3d6=12 common, 1d2=2 elites, 1 boss.

The common swarm and both elites fell without difficulty: ordinary Quickknife work under Dagger Mastery's edge, **Twin Fang's first-ever actual use** against the paired elites (both strikes landing), and a single **Flash Step** closing distance on the second elite. The boss — stone-skinned, built for the archetype's collapsing terrain — closed for one exchange: 🎲 d100 71, net +3 steps (overwhelming capability gap), success, ended in one Rupture strike. This was Rupture's **third qualifying scene at Adept**, advancing it to **Expert** (×2.30 → ×2.45, Mana 10 → 9).

**Loot:** 27 E-Rank crystals (15 from kills, 12 mined from the deposit, 3d6=12 at ×1 Rank multiplier), 3 E-Rank cores (both elites, the boss), a boss-drop equipment result (d100 34 → equipment, d20 9 → Guard Shield [E-Rank], all-Vitality budget, Vitality +2, reduction 3%, banked unequipped), and the Runic Key's guaranteed rune (d8 5 → teaches Flash Step [D-Rank] — already known, banked unconsumed).

**XP:** kill XP 12×10 (common) + 2×20 (elites) + 40 (boss) = 190, plus the E-Rank Gate-clear milestone (70) = **270 total, no underdog bonus** (creatures at Bearer's own System Rank). XP 40/800 (post-`EVT-000141` state, unaffected by that event) → wait, prior to this event XP stood at 570/800 from Checkpoint 0030; **570 + 270 = 840, crossing the 800 threshold: Level 8 → 9.** Level-up settlement: all five base Stats +1 (Strength 31→32, Agility 30→31, Vitality 30→31 [applied after the `EVT-000141` allocation], Perception 32→33, Intelligence 30→31), +5 unspent points, Health/Mana re-derived (effective Vitality 35, max Health 140; effective Intelligence 31, max Mana 62) and restored to full. Excess XP 40 carries into the 900-threshold band: **XP 40/900.**

Runic Key consumed; none remain banked."

At Alexander's request, Owen geared up in full — Bastion pieces, the Boss-Imprinted Quickknife — and took his hand as the key opened the instance around both of them, the first time Owen has crossed into one.

Run as a deliberate full-power demonstration for Owen, who was told to watch rather than fight: Rupture opened the first kill before it finished its first step; Twin Fang answered a second attacker in the same breath; Stone Skin sustained throughout (its first materially-relevant activation under genuine danger); Flash Step crossed the room once, visibly startling Owen; the elite fell to a full Rupture strike. Mana spent to zero across the display (62 → 0). Alexander materialized a Lesser Mana Potion, purchased on the spot (20 g; gold 1,302 → 1,282), and drank it in front of Owen — the first time Owen has witnessed the dimensional inventory used this plainly. Mana 0 → 16 (25% of the post-level-up maximum).

Keen Sense's second qualifying scene: 1/3 → 2/3 toward Practiced.

## EVT-000145 - The System Named: Owen Told Everything

```yaml
id: EVT-000145
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-3-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-08-04, ~20:35-20:40 -05:00, Owen Callahan's apartment"
  real_date: "2026-07-27"
type: Event
kind: disclosure
importance: pivotal
game_date: "2026-08-04, ~20:35-20:40 -05:00"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000139
progression_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
description: "Back at the apartment, food delivered and cooling, Alexander named it outright: *I am a Hunter who levels up.* He explained the mechanism in terms Owen's own comparison could hold — a status window, tracked stats, kill counts, daily quests with real deadlines and real penalties for missing them. **This is the largest and most direct disclosure of the campaign** — every prior one (the parents at the door, the pocket-gate mechanism) stopped short of naming the System itself. The System remains unknown to every other character in the campaign; Owen is now the sole exception.

Owen's reaction was processing rather than disbelief — he had just watched the mechanism operate directly. He asked practical follow-ups (what happens if a daily is skipped) rather than existential ones. Told Alexander is still 'merely' System Rank E — corrected in the same breath, since he'd misspoken; still not yet D, which needs Level 10 — Owen pointed out the mismatch between that self-assessment and what he'd just watched happen. Alexander named the fear underneath the whole project for the first time: *I was the weakest amongst weeks. I don't want to feel like that never again.* Owen reframed it back as the same instinct that once made him distrust his own correct reads, not a new flaw, and declined to tell him to stop chasing it.

Superseded: OBJ-10's premise (Owen knows something is unexplained but not what) no longer holds; see `140_OBJECTIVES.md`."
```

## EVT-000146 - Never Losing You, and the Moving-In Question

```yaml
id: EVT-000146
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-3-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-08-04, ~20:40 -05:00, Owen Callahan's apartment, over dinner"
  real_date: "2026-07-27"
type: Event
kind: social
importance: pivotal
game_date: "2026-08-04, ~20:40 -05:00"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000139
progression_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
description: "Immediately following the System's naming (`EVT-000145`), Alexander stated plainly, without visible emotion: *I am never losing you.* Owen's composure broke for the first time in this relationship — not into distress, into something softer — and he confirmed it back exactly as plainly: not leaving, wasn't planning to, and finding out the literal truth about what his boyfriend is didn't change that math.

In the same sitting, Alexander proposed moving in together. Owen's honest first reaction named the pace (five days since the relationship itself was named) before immediately confirming he wants it too, and observing he's already effectively split his time between both apartments for two weeks regardless. He stated outright what he knows he'd be agreeing to: unexplained absences, now-confirmed real danger, and being the one left wondering whether a given night ends with a story or doesn't.

Alexander pulled back mid-conversation — *let's not rush this... I am sometimes too much* — and Owen declined to accept the walk-back as a correction, naming it instead as Alexander being unpracticed at wanting things out loud rather than a flaw needing fixing. Both explicitly reaffirmed the relationship holds regardless of the pace question. Dinner, delivered earlier, was eaten over the settling silence that followed.

New objective opened: `OBJ-15` (the moving-in question), status open, no commitment made, not rejected. `REL-000066` texture and state both updated with tonight's disclosure and this exchange."
```

## EVT-000147 - Morning Run, the Daily Quest, and "I Love You Too"

```yaml
id: EVT-000147
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: session-3-gameplay
  game_date: "2026-08-05, 06:00-08:20 -05:00, Loyola Park then a diner in Rogers Park"
  real_date: "2026-07-27"

  - ENT-000139

    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
description: "The night settled deterministically under `resting` recovery from the 20:40 anchor: Mana 16→62/62 across 9h20m, Health already full. At 06:00 the System issued the twelfth daily quest. Alexander woke on schedule; Owen, asked to join, negotiated a token protest before agreeing. The regimen (100 push-ups, 100 sit-ups, 100 squats, 10 km) was completed together at Loyola Park by ~07:50 — **streak 11→12**, an ordinary (non-upgraded) Daily Random Box, one Ability Points +3 award, and one Status Recovery banked pending.

Over breakfast at a diner, Owen said, buried in a joke, that he loves Alexander — the first time either of them has used the word in any register. Alexander froze, visibly processing, then crossed the diner floor mid-walk on Sheridan Road and kissed him, saying it back plainly: *I love you too.* Both said it again, laughing, before breakfast. This is `REL-000066`'s first mutual 'I love you' — a Character First under the resident settlement rules.

Over eggs, Alexander told Owen he has a private plan for the morning: investigating whether an artificer can combine weapons into one commissioned piece, and finding any use — legal or otherwise — for the growing unsellable core stash. Owen, professionally interested, suggested the Coalition's own network (Kesha) rather than just Ada or Dale's contacts. Owen confirmed he has no Bridgeport job today, only ordinary private-patient mender rounds; the artificer lead through Kesha has not surfaced yet. The two parted at the diner, Owen for his rounds, Alexander for 'stuff.'"
```

Loot collected: **27 E-Rank crystals** (14 beast-drop + 13 mined), **4 E-Rank cores** (3 elite + 1 boss), a duplicate **Guard Shield [E-Rank]** boss drop, and the shrine's bonus roll yielding a second **Instant-Dungeon Key [E-Rank]**."
```

## EVT-000154 - Profile 1.25: Consumable Economy and Sourcing Correction

```yaml
id: EVT-000154
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: owner-ruling
  game_date: "2026-08-05 ~10:30 -05:00; profile migration, no fictional time consumed"
  real_date: "2026-07-28"
type: Event
kind: system-profile-adoption
importance: structural
game_date: "2026-08-05 ~10:30 -05:00; profile migration, no fictional time consumed"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
description: "Adopted frozen Gatefall World Rule Profile 1.25, correcting the System shop's Healing and Mana potion economy and stating their sourcing explicitly.

**Flat restoration.** Lesser and Standard potions now restore a flat amount rather than a percentage of the drinker's maximum pool: Lesser Healing 30 Health, Standard Healing 90 Health, Lesser Mana 20 Mana, Standard Mana 40 Mana, each capped at maximum. Greater Healing and Greater Mana potions continue to restore their pool to full. The change ties a lower-tier potion's value to the Gate-Rank bracket it serves (Section 11.2's potion-cache tiers) instead of letting it scale forever with the Bearer's own growth, which is what makes upgrading tiers meaningful.

**Prices.** All six potions reprice so that no tier is dominated: Healing 60/150/750 g and Mana 50/75/480 g for Lesser/Standard/Greater. Under the previous percentage model two Lessers cost less than one Standard and did the same work, so Standard and Greater were purchases no rational buyer made; now a Standard costs fewer actions and no more gold than the Lessers it replaces from D-Rank scale upward, and a Greater is the only single-action full restore. Cost-per-point does **not** fall with tier and is not meant to — what the higher tier buys is action economy. The shop's 50% unused-consumable repurchase rate is unchanged and applies to the new prices.

**Sourcing.** Healing and Mana potions are now stated to exist only inside the System shop: no licensed outfitter, guild armory, pharmacy, hospital, or black-market broker stocks one at any price, and none may appear on a world shelf, in a world loot cache, or in an NPC's kit unless Alexander put it there. The world's own restorative-alchemy market — brewed from beast cores, the thing that can arrest the gray sleep — is separate and unaffected; it acts on the severity axis over elapsed time and restores no pool as an immediate quantity. NPCs have no concept of a System potion. Owen remains the only person who knows the System exists at all.

**Migration boundary.** Every already-resolved purchase and consumption stands exactly as narrated, including this session's two Standard Mana Potions bought and drunk at the old 75 g / 50%-of-maximum terms (`EVT-000150`, `EVT-000152`) and the Lesser Mana Potions at `EVT-000144` and `EVT-000150`. No held potion changes identity, Rank, quantity, or ownership — Alexander's six banked lesser healing potions are untouched as objects; only their effect-on-use and shop price change, and only from this Event forward. The sourcing rule reopens nothing either: the two lesser restoratives Owen bought off the licensed shelf at Vanguard Trade & Gear remain his property and remain a completed purchase — they are world-market alchemy and always were, not System consumables.

No fictional time, roll, gold, cash, owned item, skill, pool, quest, damage result, or prior outcome changed. Checkpoint 0032 remains immutable at Profile 1.24 / Data Model 0.1.5 and restores through the applicable compatibility chain to Profile 1.25."
```

## EVT-000156 - Cutting Edge: The Commissioning Rules, and a Conversation That Went Nowhere

```yaml
id: EVT-000156
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: play
  game_date: "2026-08-05 ~11:20-11:32 -05:00"
  real_date: "2026-07-28"
type: Event
kind: social
importance: moderate
game_date: "2026-08-05 ~11:20-11:32 -05:00"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000149
  - ENT-000132
progression_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
description: "Alexander walked from Rogers Park to the warehouse district and entered **Cutting Edge** (`ENT-000132`) for the first time - the custom gear shop Kesha Morrison named to him on 2026-07-23 and he never followed up on. Its proprietor (`ENT-000149`, unnamed in play) is established here: mid-forties, a working artisan at a bench rather than a shopkeeper, cold and appraising, pricing a commission before a word is said.

**What he established about commissioning (consistent with Profile Section 12.8, and the first time it has been explained to Alexander in the fiction):**

- The commissioner supplies the core, and **the core's Rank is the hard ceiling** of the resulting piece's Rank.
- **Rank is not additive.** Ten E-Rank cores do not produce a D-Rank item. Combining multiple cores is a matter of **volume, not power** - more raw material means a fuller matched set or a physically larger piece at the same Rank, never a higher ceiling.
- Something genuinely out of the ordinary is a **quality** question, not a quantity one: a boss-marked core, a named item, material that is not a generic Rank drop. That kind of commission is logged twice and reviewed by people beyond the shop, especially near B-Rank and above.
- He is **licensed and rigid about it**: he logs a core's provenance before it touches anything carrying his mark, and will not bend on that for anyone. Fine for clean material; useless for Alexander's private stash.

Alexander declined, three times, to state how many cores he holds or where they came from, and countered the proprietor's pressure by pointing out he had been asked to disclose his resources without being shown anything (d100 63 - partial success: it bought patience rather than compliance; the proprietor conceded the framing, held his own line on why provenance matters to him legally, and made a narrower offer - put one core physically on the counter and he would say what could be done with it, no interrogation). Alexander refused that too, told him to come back when he wanted to work rather than vent, and left.

**Nothing was bought, sold, commissioned, or promised.** The door is explicitly left open on both sides. No cores, gold, cash, or items changed hands; no relationship identifier is minted, as no standing arrangement was formed."
```

## EVT-000157 - The Pawnbroker, Round Two: Cores for Information, and an Artificer

```yaml
id: EVT-000157
canonical_record: REC-000079
schema_version: "0.1.5"
status: active
provenance:
  source: play
  game_date: "2026-08-05 ~11:40-12:15 -05:00"
  real_date: "2026-07-28"
type: Event
kind: social
importance: major
game_date: "2026-08-05 ~11:40-12:15 -05:00"
participants:
  - ENT-000125
  - ENT-000147
progression_audits:
  - subject: ENT-000125
    domain: gatefall.skill_formation
    result: none
description: "Alexander walked the short distance to the unnamed warehouse-district pawn shop (`ENT-000147`) - timing his entry between a delivery truck and a group of dockworkers so as not to be seen going in - and converted a one-off sale into a working arrangement (`REL-000069`).

**The proposition.** He stated plainly that an ongoing relationship benefits both of them and that trust is earned by doing rather than waiting. The pawnbroker took it well and described how he actually runs the grey side of his business: a private handwritten tally with a mark rather than a name, so the ledger points nowhere if the shop is ever visited; fair quotes; no questions past what he needs to move goods safely; and consistency valued above any single windfall.

**His want-list, volunteered when asked what he actually wants rather than what he'll take: C-Rank cores and above.** He nearly gave a reason, visibly caught himself, and said only that he has a use for things that don't come cheap through normal channels and that the shop's margins don't stretch to it. **This is an unresolved thread - he wants high-Rank cores for something he declined to name.** Alexander answered that he cannot promise to hold onto that kind of material - *yet* - and the pawnbroker filed the word without pursuing it, consistent with the discretion he showed at the dimensional-inventory slip on 2026-08-04.

**The trade.** Prices quoted: district gossip free with a first real sale (nominally 2 E-Rank crystals); BGM's actual flagging mechanics 4 E-Rank cores; Cicero's Quiet's real provenance 5 E-Rank cores - **9 cores for the priced pair.** Alexander drew **ten E-Rank cores** out of the dimensional inventory through his usual concealment - reaching into the ordinary backpack he carries for exactly this purpose, so nothing uncanny was visible - and paid one over the asking price deliberately, as a bonus rather than a haggle. The pawnbroker registered the gesture as the trust-by-doing Alexander had pitched, and gave all three pieces without further negotiation.

**1. Who to trust in the warehouse district.** Cutting Edge (`ENT-000132`) is straight and does good work but is rigidly licensed and will always log provenance. **Ferro's Reclamation** (`ENT-000150`, two blocks north, no relation to Denise Ferro) *talks*: its owner trades favors with Coalition compliance staff in both directions. Nothing murky should be sold there and nothing said there that Alexander would not want repeated to a badge. Everyone else in the district is neutral and keeps to itself.

**2. How BGM actually catches undeclared goods.** There is no tracer on a crystal and no core detector. The flag is **paperwork**: the licensed market runs on declared Gate, contractor, split sheet, and appraiser sign-off, and an item presented for licensed resale with no trail behind it is the anomaly - not the item itself. Spot audits fire when **declared volume looks wrong against a hunter's recorded clears.** The other half is **informants**, the Reclamation owner among them. Practical upshot for Alexander: the risk is paperwork gaps and people talking, not detection technology.

**3. Cicero's Quiet's real provenance.** The polite version ('pulled from the quarantined Cicero Scar by a cordon scavenger') is true but incomplete. Cordon work during an active quarantine is **illegal outright** - whoever recovered the dagger was unlicensed and had no business near that Scar. The pawnbroker bought it through a middleman and never met the scavenger. Per that middleman, **the scavenger stopped taking calls not long after the sale** - no traced trouble, no body, nothing official, simply gone quiet. The phrase 'came out colder than it went in' is the middleman's own words, not shop poetry. **This is an unresolved thread and is now Character Knowledge only** - an unverified secondhand account, not established Objective Reality about the artifact or the scavenger's fate.

**On Cicero itself**, asked directly: still federally cordoned and unchanged in years. Edges reclaimed enough for BGM-escorted survey and harvest crews; the core blocks never revert and are not cleared by anyone, there being no boss left - a managed wound rather than a Gate. The circulating unofficial account: perimeter sensors log unexplained periodic mana spikes on no published schedule, and survey teams report beast remains bearing **old healed wounds**, which should not exist on a creature that came out of a Gate with no history. Nothing official confirms it; nobody who has done the walk denies it. (This matches the authoritative world record `ENT-000091` in `worlds/gatefall/210_PLACES.md`; it is recorded here as knowledge Alexander now holds, not as new world canon.)

**The artificer.** Alexander asked for a good artificer. The pawnbroker was genuinely surprised, and named it the largest favor of the conversation: the good licensed ones do not take walk-ins who will not state a core's origin, but he knows one who is **not licensed the way the guild would prefer**, does real quality work, and has never asked him an unnecessary question. Making the introduction means putting his own name on Alexander personally. He agreed to arrange it as a **consultation rather than a commission**, on Alexander's own preference - the honest pitch, since Alexander does not yet know what he wants built. Expected cost: **$200-300 cash for an hour**, possibly waived as a favor; the artificer takes neither gold nor crystals, and any actual commission later is a separate negotiation. Timing is the artificer's own - a day, or several. **The introduction is pending, not made; the artificer is referenced but unmet and unnamed, and no identifier is minted for him.**

**What Alexander actually wants to ask him**, stated aloud to the pawnbroker: whether **beast parts can be infused into weapons and gear**, and whether **multiple mana items can be combined into one.** The pawnbroker's honest answer to the first half is that beast materials in gear is not exotic at all - hide, fang and scale are stock commissioning ingredients (Profile Section 12.8) and any competent artificer already uses them; fusing them past the ordinary stat budget, or merging separate mana items into a single item with combined properties, is past what a broker knows. **Both questions are open and now carried into the pending consultation.**

**Settlement.** Private E-Rank core stash **22 -> 12**. No gold, cash, crystal, equipment, Stat, XP, Health, quest, or skill counter changed. No die resolved anything in this scene. Alexander closed by confirming he would return with goods for the artificer's answer."
```
